class="category-sub-scrollDown"></span></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="w-block w-rte" id="wpid29096188"><div class="w-holder"><p>AKCIJSKA CENA - Cena, ki jo za izdelek plača vsak kupec. V primeru, da izdelek nima akcijske cene, kupec plača redno ceno izdelka. KLUB CENA - Cena, ki jo za izdelek plača član kluba. POPUST - Popust, ki se obračuna na redno ceno izdelka, in velja za vse kupce. KLUB POPUST - Popust, ki se obračuna na redno ceno izdelka, in velja za člane kluba. KLUB BONUS - Vrednost popusta, ki se obračuna na redno ceno izdelka, in se prišteje na klubsko kartico. SPLETNA AKCIJA - pri izdelkih označenih z ikonico "Spletna akcija" klub bonus, akcijska cena, popust, vrednost s popustom veljajo samo za nakupe v spletni trgovini. Akcije se med seboj izključujejo. Akcije ne veljajo za izdelke blagovne znamke Stokke ter vsa začetna mleka.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="d-wrapper footer" data-id="d_5" style="min-height:50px;"><div         function AjaxQueue() {
            var currentIndex = 1;
            var currentPost = null;
            this.push = function (url, data, callback) {
                var ind = ++currentIndex;
                if (currentPost) {
                    currentPost.abort();
                }
                currentPost = $.post(url, data, function (res) {
                    if (ind == currentIndex) {
                        callback(res);
                    }
                }, "json");
            }
        }

        function handleLoginForm(data) {
            if (data.success) {
                ga('send', {
                    hitType: 'event',
                    eventCategory: 'Login',
                    eventAction: "Success",
                    hitCallback: function () {
                        if ($(".login-reload").size() == 1) {
                            location.reload(true);
                        } else {
                            location.href = data.url;
                        }
                    }
                });
            } else {
                ga('send', {
                    hitType: 'event',
                    eventCategory: 'Login',
                    eventAction: "Error"
                });
            }
        }

            function trackEnhanced(price, qty) {
                if (!window["GAproductImpression"]) {
                    return;
                }
                var p = window["GAproductImpression"];
                p.price = price;
                p.quantity = qty;
                ga('ec:addProduct', p);
                ga('ec:setAction', 'add');
                ga('send', 'event', 'UX', 'click', 'add to cart');
            }

            if (!toCart.is(".disabled")) {
                var pPrice = '0';
                $(".price-holder .loader").css({opacity: 0.7}).show();
                toCart.addClass("loading");
                if (bundle) {
                    $(".bundle-product:visible", bundle).each(function () {
                        var p = $(this);
                        bundleProducts.push(p.data("id"));
                        fbq('track', 'AddToCart', {
                            content_ids: ["" + p.data("id")],
                            content_type: 'product',
                            value: parseFloat(p.data("price")),
                            currency: 'EUR'
                        });
                    });
                } else {
                    fbq('track', 'AddToCart', {
                        content_ids: ["" + basket.val()],
                        content_type: 'product',
                        value: parseFloat(pPrice) * qty,
                        currency: 'EUR'
                    });
                }
                $.post(location.href, {
                    _toCart: basket.val(), qty: qty,
                    bundle: bundle ? true : false,
                    bundleProducts: bundleProducts
                }, function (data) {
                    toCart.removeClass("loading");
                    if (data.redirect) {
                        trackEnhanced(parseFloat(pPrice), qty);
                        location.href = data.redirect;
                    } else if (data.error) {
                        $('<div class="error">' + data.error + "</div>").uiNotification({
                            orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                            right: $(".d-wrapper > .d-container:eq(0)").offset().left
                        });
                    } else {
                        trackEnhanced(parseFloat(pPrice), qty);
                        var holder = $("<div></div>");
                        holder.append($(data.success));
                        holder.append($('<a href="' + data.cartLink + '">' + data.cartTit + '</a>'));
                        holder.uiNotification({
                            orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                            right: $(".d-wrapper > .d-container:eq(0)").offset().left
                        });
                        $(".cart-count").text(data.cartCount);
                    }
                    $(".price-holder .loader").hide();
                }, "json");
            } else {
                $("<div>" + text.selectOptions + "</div>").uiNotification({
                    orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                    right: $(".d-wrapper > .d-container:eq(0)").offset().left
                });
            }
            return false;
        });

        if ($(".cart-final").length > 0 && $(".finalStep-button").length > 0) {
            $(".cart-count").text("0");
        }

        $(".cart-table-qty .incr").click(function () {

            }
            val--;
            if (val < 0) {
                val = 0;
            }
            $(".val", parent).val(val).trigger("blur");
        });

        function refreshLazy() {
            $(".lazy-img").each(function () {
                var img = $(this), o1 = img.offset();
                if (o1.top < $(window).scrollTop() + $(window).height()) {
                    img.attr("src", img.data("src")).removeClass("lazy-img");
                }
            });
        }

        $(window).scroll(refreshLazy);
        refreshLazy();

        function filterProducts(fn) {
            var priceFilterLow, priceFilterHigh;
            if ($(".sub-category .ui-slider").length > 0) {
                priceFilterLow = $(".sub-category .ui-slider-left").data("val");
                priceFilterHigh = $(".sub-category .ui-slider-right").data("val");
            }

            $(".product-list .product-thumb").each(function () {
                if (fn(this)) {
                    $(this).show();
                } else {
                    $(this).hide();
                }

                var price = parseFloat($(this).data("price"));
                if ((priceFilterLow && price < priceFilterLow) || (priceFilterHigh && price > priceFilterHigh)) {
                    $(this).hide();
                }

            });
            refreshLazy();
        }

        function buildFilterHash() {
            var checked = $(".group-holder input:checked");
            var hash = "";
            var obj = {};
            checked.each(function () {
                var name = $(this).attr("name");
                if (!obj[name]) {
                    obj[name] = [];
                }
                obj[name].push($(this).val())
            });

            Object.keys(obj).forEach(function (key) {
                hash += ";filter:" + key + ":" + obj[key].join(",");
            });
            hash += ";sort:" + $(".sort-bar select").val();
            window.location.hash = hash;
        }

        function parseHash() {
            var hash = window.location.hash || "";
            var hashSplit = hash.split(";");
            for (var i = 0; i < hashSplit.length; i++) {
                var parts = hashSplit[i].split(":");
                if (parts[0] == "filter") {
                    var ids = parts[2].split(",");
                    $(".group-holder input[name=" + parts[1] + "]").each(function () {
                        var val = $(this).attr("value");
                        if (ids.indexOf(val) != -1) {
                            $(this).prop("checked", true).trigger("change");
                        }
                    });
                } else if (parts[0] == "sort") {
                    var direction = parts[1];
                    if (direction) {
                        $(".sort-bar select").val(direction).trigger("updateUi");
                        sort(direction);
                    }
                }

            }
        }

        parseHash();

        $(".group-holder input").change(function () {
            if ($(this).closest(".toys-filter").length == 0) {
                var filter = {
                    brand: [],
                    size: [],
                    group: [],
                    pricespecial: []
                };
                var checked = $(".group-holder input:checked");
                if (checked.size() == 0) {
                    filterProducts(function () {
                        return true;
                    });
                } else {
                    checked.each(function () {
                        if (filter.hasOwnProperty($(this).attr("name"))) {
                            filter[$(this).attr("name")].push($(this).val());
                        } else {
                            filter[$(this).attr("name")] = [];
                            filter[$(this).attr("name")].push($(this).val());
                        }
                        if ($(this).data("multi")) {
                            var m = $(this).data("multi").toString().split(",");
                            filter[$(this).attr("name")] = filter[$(this).attr("name")].concat(m);
                        }
                    });
                    filterProducts(function (p) {
                        var filterExists = false, $p = $(p), visible = true, i1;
                        for (var f1 in filter) {
                            if (filter.hasOwnProperty(f1)) {
                                var b1 = $p.data(f1), found = false;
                                if (filter[f1].length > 0) {
                                    for (i1 = 0; i1 < filter[f1].length; i1++) {
                                        if (b1 == filter[f1][i1]) {
                                            found = true;
                                        } else if (("" + b1).indexOf(filter[f1][i1]) != -1) {
                                            found = true;
                                        }
                                    }
                                    if (!found) {
                                        visible = false;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        return visible;
                    });
                }
                buildFilterHash();
            }
        }).eq(0).trigger("change");


        $(".sort-bar select").change(function () {
            sort($(this).val());
        });

        function sort(checked) {
            var list = $(".product-list .product-thumb").toArray();
            if (checked == "nameAsc") {
                list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var a1 = $(a).data("name").toLowerCase();
                    var b1 = $(b).data("name").toLowerCase();
                    if (a1 < b1) {
                        return 1;
                    } else if (a1 > b1) {
                        return -1;
                    } else {
                        return 0;
                    }
                });
            } else if (checked == "nameDesc") {
                list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var a1 = $(a).data("name").toLowerCase();
                    var b1 = $(b).data("name").toLowerCase();
                    if (a1 < b1) {
                        return -1;
                    } else if (a1 > b1) {
                        return 1;
                    } else {
                        return 0;
                    }
                });
            } else if (checked == "priceAsc") {
                list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var a1 = parseFloat($(a).data("price"));
                    var b1 = parseFloat($(b).data("price"));
                    if (a1 < b1) {
                        return 1;
                    } else if (a1 > b1) {
                        return -1;
                    } else {
                        return 0;
                    }
                });
            } else if (checked == "priceDesc") {
                list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var a1 = parseFloat($(a).data("price"));
                    var b1 = parseFloat($(b).data("price"));
                    if (a1 < b1) {
                        return -1;
                    } else if (a1 > b1) {
                        return 1;
                    } else {
                        return 0;
                    }
                });
            } else if (checked == "default") {
                list.sort(function (a, b) {
                    var a1 = parseInt($(a).data("position"));
                    var b1 = parseInt($(b).data("position"));
                    if (a1 < b1) {
                        return 1;
                    } else if (a1 > b1) {
                        return -1;
                    } else {
                        return 0;
                    }
                });
            }
            list.reverse();
            for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
                $(".product-list").append(list[i]);
            }
            refreshLazy();
            buildFilterHash();
        }

        $("select[name=contactId]").change(function () {
            var val = $(this).val();

            function updateValues(contact) {
                $(".value-firstName").text(contact["PCON_NAME"]);
                $(".value-lastName").text(contact["PCON_SURNAME"]);
                $(".value-street").text(contact["PCON_STREET"]);
                $(".value-city").text(contact["POST_CITY"]);
                $(".value-postal").text(contact["POST_CODE"] + ' ' + contact["POST_CITY"]);

                $(".value-birthDate").text(contact["PCON_BIRTHDAY"]);
                $(".value-country").text(contact["COUN_NAMESMALL"]);


                $("[name=firstName]").val(contact["PCON_NAME"]);
                $("[name=lastName]").val(contact["PCON_SURNAME"]);
                $("[name=street]").val(contact["PCON_STREET"]);
//                $("[name=houseNumber, .value-]").val(contact["PCON_POBOX"]);
                $("[name=city]").val(contact["POST_CITY"]);
                $("[name=postal]").val(contact["POST_CODE"]).trigger("change");
                $("[name=phone]").val(contact["PCON_PHONEHOME"]);
                $("[name=phoneMobile]").val(contact["PCON_PHONEMOBILE"]);
                $("[name=birthDate]").val(contact["PCON_BIRTHDAY"]);

                var country = $("[name=country]");
                var countryVal = "";
                country.find("option").each(function () {
                    var sp = $(this).attr("value").split(",");
                    if (sp[1] == contact["COUN_CODE"]) {
                        countryVal = $(this).attr("value");
                    }
                });
                country.val(countryVal).trigger("change");
                var countryVal2 = country.val().split(",");
                $(".value-phone").text(contact["PCON_PHONEHOME"]);
                $(".value-phoneMobile").text(contact["PCON_PHONEMOBILE"]);
            }

            if (val == "new") {
                $(".contact-view").hide();
                $(".contact-form").show();
                updateValues(userContacts[0]);
            } else {
                $(".contact-view").show();
                $(".contact-form").hide();
                for (var i = 0; i < userContacts.length; i++) {
                    if (userContacts[i]["PCON_ID"] == val) {
                        updateValues(userContacts[i]);
                    }
                }
            }

        }).trigger("change");

        function contactFormV2(contact) {
            var form = $(contact).find("form");

            var address = null;
            form.on({
                "form.submit": function (e, data) {
                    var addresses = $(".addresses .address");
                    address = addresses.eq(0).clone();
                    if (address.find(".remove").length == 0) {
                        address.append($('<span class="remove">X</span>'));
                    }
                    address.find(".value-firstName").text($(contact).find("input[name=firstName]").val());
                    address.find(".value-lastName").text($(contact).find("input[name=lastName]").val());
                    address.find(".value-street").text($(contact).find("input[name=street]").val());
                    address.find(".value-country").text($(contact).find("select[name=country]").val());
                    address.find(".value-postal").text($(contact).find("select[name=postal]").val());
                },
                "form.complete": function (e, data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        address.find("input").val(data.pcon);
                        $(".addresses-holder .addresses").prepend(address).css({width: (195 * $(".addresses-holder .address").length) + "px"});
                        address.find(".ui-checkbox").uiCheckbox();
                        address.find('.ui-checkbox input').prop("checked", true).trigger("change");
                        address.show();
                        address.find(".remove").show();
                        $(contact).find("select").trigger("change");
                    }
                }
            });
        }

        function cartV2nextStep(cb) {                                   //TODO error if not selected
            var data = {
                deliveryType: 1
            };
            var chooseAddress = $(".cart-chooseAddress");
            if (chooseAddress.length > 0) {
                data.contact = $('input[name=contact]:checked', chooseAddress).val();
            }
            var delivery = $(".cart-delivery");
            if (delivery.length > 0) {
                data.shipmentType = $('input[name=shipmentType]:checked', delivery).val();
            }
            var comment = $(".cart-comment");
            if (comment.length > 0) {
                data.comment = $('textarea', comment).val();
            }
            var payment = $(".cart-payment");
            if (payment.length > 0) {
                data.payment = $('input[name=paymentType]:checked', payment).val();
            }
            var guestAddress = $(".enterAddress .contactForm.addNewUser");
            if (guestAddress.length > 0) {
                var form = guestAddress.find("form");
                if (!form.hasClass("inited")) {
                    form.addClass("inited").on({
                        "form.complete": function (e, data2) {
                            if (data2.success) {
                                cartV2loader.trigger("loader.start");
                                data.contact = data2.pcon;
                                $.post(location.href, {_form: "cartNextStep", data: data}, function (data) {
                                    cb(data);
                                }, "json").fail(function (e) {
                                    location.href = "?cartV2=5&merr=err02-" + e.status + '-' + encodeURIComponent(e.responseText);
                                });
                            } else {
                                if (data2.error) {
                                    var error = form.find(".ui-form-error");
                                    if (error.length > 0) {
                                        error.text(data2.error);
                                    } else {
                                        form.append('<div class="ui-form-error" style="display:block">' + data2.error + '</div>');
                                    }
                                    cb({invalid: true});
                                } else {
                                    cb({});
                                }
                            }
                        },
                        "form.invalid": function (e) {
                            cb({invalid: true});
                        }
                    });
                }
                guestAddress.find("form").submit();
            } else {
                $.post(location.href, {_form: "cartNextStep", data: data}, function (data) {
                    cb(data);
                }, "json").fail(function (e) {
                    location.href = "?cartV2=5&merr=err03-" + e.status + '-' + encodeURIComponent(e.responseText);
                });
            }
        }

        function cartV2placeOrder() {
            $.post(location.href, {_form: "cartPlaceOrder"}, function (data) {
                if (data.success) {
                                        var wspay = $("#bankart");
                                        if (wspay.size() > 0) {
                        wspay.submit();
                    } else {
                        location.href = "?cartV2=4";
                    }
                } else {
                    location.href = "?cartV2=5" + (data.error ? "&merr=err04-" + data.error : '');
                }
            }, "json").fail(function (e) {
                location.href = "?cartV2=5&merr=err01-" + e.status + '-' + encodeURIComponent(e.responseText);
            });
        }

        var cartV2loader;
        $(".content-cart").each(function () {            //cartV2
            cartV2loader = $('.content-cart').addClass("loader").uiLoader();
        });

        var nextStepLinks = $(".cart-nextStep a, .cart-progress a");
        nextStepLinks.on({         //cartV2
            click: function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();
                var nStep = parseInt($(this).attr("href").slice(-1));
                var cStep = parseInt(document.location.search.slice(-1));
                if (nStep < cStep) {
                    location.href = $(this).attr("href");
                    return;
                } else if (nStep == cStep) {
                    return false;
                }

                var a = $(this);
                if (a.hasClass("disabled")) {
                    return false;
                }
                nextStepLinks.addClass("disabled");

                if (cartV2loader) {
                    cartV2loader.trigger("loader.start");
                }
                cartV2nextStep(function (data) {
                    if (data.success) {
                        var cartNextStep = $(".cart-nextStep");
                        if (cartNextStep.length > 0 && cartNextStep.eq(0).hasClass("cart-placeOrder")) {
                            cartV2placeOrder();
                        } else {
                            location.href = a.attr("href");
                        }
                    } else if (data.invalid) {
                        if (cartV2loader) {
                            cartV2loader.trigger("loader.stop");
                        }
                        nextStepLinks.removeClass("disabled");
                    } else {
                        location.reload(true);
                    }
                });
                return false;
            }
        });

        $(".contactForm").each(function () {
            if ($(this).parents(".cart-addAddress").length > 0) {
                contactFormV2(this);
                return;
            }

            if (phpUser) {
                var contact = this;
                var wrap = $('<div class="showContactForm"><div class="showContactFormButton">' + text.addNewContact + '</div></div>');
                var form = $(' <form action="." method="post" name="addContactForm" class="form-delivery ui-form" style="display:none"></form>');
                wrap.append(form);
                wrap.find(".showContactFormButton").on({
                    click: function () {
                        $(this).hide();
                        form.show();
                        updateFormMetrics();
                    }
                });
                var parentForm = $(this).closest("form");
                parentForm.parent().css({position: "relative"});
                wrap.insertAfter(parentForm);
                var heightHolder = $('<div class="heightHolder">').insertAfter(this);

                function updateFormMetrics() {
                    heightHolder.css({
                        float: "left",
                        height: $(contact).height() + "px",
                        width: $(contact).width() + 'px'
                    });
                    wrap.css({
                        position: "absolute", top: heightHolder.position().top + "px",
                        left: heightHolder.position().left + "px"
                    });
                }

                form.append($(contact));
                updateFormMetrics();
                $('.contactFormButtonSubmit', contact).show().text($('.contactFormButton', contact).hide().text()).addClass("contactFormButton promoButton");
                setInterval(updateFormMetrics, 1000);
                form.uiForm();
                var address = null;
                form.on({
                    "form.submit": function (e, data) {
                        var addresses = $(".addresses .address");
                        address = addresses.eq(0).clone();
                        address.find(".value-firstName").text($(contact).find("input[name=firstName]").val());
                        address.find(".value-lastName").text($(contact).find("input[name=lastName]").val());
                        address.find(".value-street").text($(contact).find("input[name=street]").val());
                        address.find(".value-country").text($(contact).find("select[name=country]").val());
                        address.find(".value-postal").text($(contact).find("select[name=postal]").val());
                    },
                    "form.complete": function (e, data) {
                        if (data.success) {
                            address.find("input").val(data.pcon);
                            $(".addresses-holder .addresses").prepend(address).css({width: (195 * $(".addresses-holder .address").length) + "px"});
                            address.find(".ui-checkbox").uiCheckbox();
                            address.find('.ui-checkbox input').prop("checked", true).trigger("change");
                            address.show();
                            address.find(".remove").show();
                            $(contact).find("select").trigger("change");
                        }
                    }
                });
            }
        });


        $(".side-holder").each(function () {
            var holder = $(this).css({width: $(this).parent().width() + "px"});
            var content = $(".content");
            var tm;

            function updateH() {
                if (holder.parent().offset().top < $(window).scrollTop()) {
//                $(".side").css({position:"relative"});
                    var t = $(window).scrollTop() + 10;
                    if (content.offset().top + content.height() < t + holder.height()) {
                        t = content.offset().top + content.height() - holder.height();
                    }
                    $(".side-holder").css({position: "absolute", top: t + "px"});
                } else {
                    $(".side-holder").css({position: "", top: ""});
                }
            }


            $(window).on("scroll", function () {
                updateH();
            });
//        tm = setInterval(updateH, 300);
        });

        $(".sideCart .product .remove").click(function () {
            var product = $(this).closest(".product");
//            console.log($(".package" + product.data("id"), product.parent()).length);
//            return;
            $.post(location.href, {_form: "removeFromCart", id: product.data("id")}, function (data) {
                try {
                    data = JSON.parse(data);
                } catch (e) {
                    data = {};
                }
                if (data.success) {
                    $(".package" + product.data("id"), product.parent()).remove();
                    product.remove();
                    $(".sideCart .total .price").text(data.due);
                    $(".sideCart .bonus .gainedBonus .price").text(data.bonus);
                    $(".sideCart .bonus .discount .price").text(data.discount);

                    if ($(".products .product").size() == 0) {
                        $(".side-nextStep").remove();
                    }
                } else {
                    product.find(".error").remove();
                    product.append('<span class="error">' + data.error + '</span>');
                }
            });
        });

        function updateScrollSub(b1, t) {
            var p1 = $(b1).closest(".thumb-holder"), l1 = p1.find(".category-info-sub");
            $(".category-sub-scrollUp", p1).css({opacity: t > 0 ? 1 : 0.3});
            $(".category-sub-scrollDown", p1).css({opacity: t < l1.prop("scrollHeight") - 102 ? 1 : 0.3});
        }

        $(".category-sub-scrollUp").click(function (e) {
            var p = $(this).closest(".thumb-holder").find(".category-info-sub"), t1 = p.scrollTop() - 102;
            updateScrollSub(this, t1);
            p.scrollTop(t1);
        });
        $(".category-sub-scrollDown").click(function (e) {
            var p = $(this).closest(".thumb-holder").find(".category-info-sub"), t1 = p.scrollTop() + 102;
            updateScrollSub(this, t1);
            p.scrollTop(t1);
        });
        $(".category-sub-scrollDown, .category-sub-scrollUp").mousedown(function (e) {

        $(".tpl-toys-products .filterSecond .group-holder input").change(function () {
            var filter = $(this).closest(".filterSecond").find(".toys-filter");
            var labels = filter.find(".labels");
            if ($(this).is(":checked")) {
                labels.append($('<span class="label-holder" data-value="' + $(this).attr("value") + '"><span class="label-title">' + $(this).closest("label").text() + '</span><span class="label-close">x</span></span>'))
            } else {
                labels.find('span[data-value="' + $(this).attr("value") + '"]').remove();
            }
            tplToysFilters();
        });
        function tplToysFilters() {
            var filter = {
                brand: []
            };
            var checked = $(".tpl-toys-products .labels .label-holder");
            if (checked.size() == 0) {
                filterProducts(function (p) {
                    var filterExists = false, $p = $(p), visible = true;
                    var b1 = $p.data("price"), found = false;
                    if (parseFloat(b1) <= parseFloat($(".tpl-toys-products .filter-toys-slider .ui-slider-handleRight .filter-price-text").text()) &&
                        parseFloat(b1) >= parseFloat($(".tpl-toys-products .filter-toys-slider .ui-slider-handleLeft .filter-price-text").text())) {
                        found = true;
                    }
                    if (!found) {
                        visible = false;
                    }
                    return visible;
                });
            } else {
                checked.each(function () {
                    if (filter.hasOwnProperty("brand")) {
                        filter["brand"].push($(this).data("value"));
                    }
                });
                filterProducts(function (p) {
                    var filterExists = false, $p = $(p), visible = true, i1;
                    for (var f1 in filter) {
                        if (filter.hasOwnProperty(f1)) {
                            var b1 = $p.data(f1), b2 = $p.data("price"), found = false;
                            if (filter[f1].length > 0) {
                                for (i1 = 0; i1 < filter[f1].length; i1++) {
                                    if (b1 == filter[f1][i1] && parseFloat(b2) <= parseFloat($(".tpl-toys-products .filter-toys-slider .ui-slider-handleRight .filter-price-text").text()) &&
                                        parseFloat(b2) >= parseFloat($(".tpl-toys-products .filter-toys-slider .ui-slider-handleLeft .filter-price-text").text())) {
                                        found = true;
                                    }
                                }
                                if (!found) {
                                    visible = false;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return visible;
                });
            }
        }

        $(".tpl-toys-products .labels").on({
            click: function () {
                var filter = $(this).closest(".filterSecond").find(".filter-toys-brand-holder");
                filter.find('input[value="' + $(this).parent().data("value") + '"]').trigger("click");
                $(this).parent().remove();
                tplToysFilters();
            }
        }, ".label-close");
        $(".tpl-toys-products .toys-filter .ui-slider .ui-slider-handleLeft, .tpl-toys-products .toys-filter .ui-slider .ui-slider-handleRight").on({

                $(this).find(".ui-slider-handleRight .filter-price-text").text(priceMax);
                var priceMin = Math.round((parseFloat($(this).data("maxprice")) / 100) * $(this).find(".ui-slider-min").val());
                $(this).find(".ui-slider-handleLeft .filter-price-text").text(priceMin);
                tplToysFilters();
            }
        });

        $(".track-toCart").click(function () {
            var id = $(this).data('id');
            ga('send', {
                hitType: 'event',
                eventCategory: 'track-toCart',
                eventAction: "" + id
            });
            $.post(location.href, {_toCart: id, qty: 1}, function (data) {
                if (data.error) {
                    $('<div class="error">' + data.error + "</div>").uiNotification({
                        orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                        right: $(".body-wrapper > .container:eq(0)").offset().left
                    });
                } else {
                    var holder = $("<div></div>");
                    holder.append($(data.success));
                    holder.append($('<a href="' + data.cartLink + '">' + data.cartTit + '</a>'));
                    holder.uiNotification({
                        orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                        right: $(".body-wrapper > .container:eq(0)").offset().left
                    });
                }
            }, "json");
            return false;
        });
        $(".product-list").on({
            click: function (e) {
                var thumb = $(this).closest(".thumb");
                if (thumb.size() == 0) {
                    return;
                }
                var selected = $('.group-filter-SIZE input:checked');
                var vals = [];
                var href = "";
                selected.each(function () {
                    var val = $(this).val();
                    var link = thumb.find(".size-link-" + val);
                    if (!href && link.size() > 0) {
                        href = link.attr("href");
                    }
                });
                if (href) {
                    location.href = href;
                    e.preventDefault();
                }
            }
        }, "a");

        function changeBundle(id) {
            bundleLoader.trigger("loader.start");
            var $products = $(this).closest(".bundle-products");
            $(".bundle-product", $products).hide();
            $('.bundle-product[data-id="' + id + '"]').eq(0).show();

            var holder = $(".bi-productPrices .price-holder");
            var bundleProducts = [
                {
                    PRICE: holder.data("price"),
                    PRICE_OLD: holder.data("price_old"),
                    DISCOUNT_PRICE: holder.data("price_discount")
                }
            ];

            $(".bundle .bundle-product:visible").each(function () {
                bundleProducts.push({
                    PRICE: $(this).data("price"),
                    PRICE_OLD: $(this).data("price_old"),
                    DISCOUNT_PRICE: $(this).data("price_discount")
                })
            });

            $.post(location.href, {_bundleProducts: bundleProducts}, function (data) {
                if (data.success) {
                    var wrapper = $('<div>' + data.success + '</div>');
                    holder.find(".price-small,.price-large").remove();
                    holder.find(".loader").after(wrapper.contents());
                }
                bundleLoader.trigger("loader.stop");
            }, "json");
        }

        $(".sub-category-grid a").on({
            click: function (e) {
                var a = $(this);
                var id = $(this).closest(".product-thumb").data("id");
                if (!window["GAproductImpressions"] || !window["GAproductImpressions"][id]) {
                    return true;
                }
                var list = window["GAproductImpressions"][id].list;
                delete window["GAproductImpressions"][id].list;
                ga('ec:addProduct', window["GAproductImpressions"][id]);
                ga('ec:setAction', 'click', {list: list});
                ga('send', 'event', 'UX', 'click', 'Results', {
                    hitCallback: function () {
                        document.location = a.attr("href");
                    }
                });
                return false;
            }
        });

                        $("form[name=contact] input[name=firstName]", step3).each(function () {
                            if ($(this).val()) {
                                isOpt106Required = true;
                            }
                        });
                        if (isOpt106Required) {
                            var i106form = i106.closest("form");
                            i106.data("required", true).data("msg_required", t("l.register.optionRequired106"));
                            var ui = i106form.data("ui");
                            if (!ui.validate()) {
                                return;
                            }
                        }
                        $("form", tabs).each(function () {
                            var ui = $(this).data("ui");
                            var name = $(this).attr("name");
                            var uiData = ui.getData();
                            if (name == "userOptions") {
                                if (Array.isArray(uiData["agreeOption[]"])) {
                                    data.options = data.options.concat(uiData["agreeOption[]"]);
                                } else {
                                    data.options.push(uiData["agreeOption[]"]);
                                }
                            } else if (name == "contact") {
                                data.contacts.push(uiData);
                            }
                        });
                        tabs.trigger("loader.start");
                        $.post(location.href, {
                            _form: "gdprData",
                            agreeOption: data.options,
                            contacts: data.contacts
                        }, function (data) {
                            if (data.success) {
                                $(".ui-tabs-header li", tabs).eq(3).find("a").trigger("click");
                                $(window).scrollTop(tabs.offset().top - 120);
                                tabs.trigger("loader.stop");
                            } else if (data.error) {
                                tabs.trigger("loader.stop");
                                $('<div class="error">' + data.error + "</div>").uiNotification({
                                    orientation: "top", timeout: 5000,
                                    right: $(".d-wrapper > .d-container:eq(0)").offset().left
                                });
                            }
                        }, "json");
                    }
                }
            });

        });


        $(".filterOdprodaja").each(function () {
            var $elm = $(this);
            var filters = {
                poslovalnica: {},
                vrsta: {}
            };
            $(".w-menu-gridThumb", $(this)).each(function () {
                filters.poslovalnica[$(this).data("poslovalnica")] = 1;
                filters.vrsta[$(this).data("vrsta")] = 1;
            });

            var $filters = $('<div class="filters"></div>');
            var $filter = $('<div class="filter"><span class="ui-select"><select><option value="all" selected="selected"></option></select></span></div>');

            var $poslovalnica = $filter.clone();
            $("select", $poslovalnica).attr("name", "poslovalnica");
            $("option", $poslovalnica).text(t("l.filter.allShops"));
            Object.keys(filters.poslovalnica).forEach(function (key) {
                $("select", $poslovalnica).append($('<option>' + key + '</option>'));
            });
            $poslovalnica.appendTo($filters);

            var $vrsta = $filter.clone().data("filtertype", "vrsta");
            $("select", $vrsta).attr("name", "vrsta");
            $("option", $vrsta).text(t("l.filter.allCategories"));
            Object.keys(filters.vrsta).forEach(function (key) {
                $("select", $vrsta).append($('<option>' + key + '</option>'));
            });
            $vrsta.appendTo($filters);

            $filters.prependTo($elm);

            $(".ui-select", $elm).uiSelect();
            $("select", $elm).on({
                change: function () {
                    var poslovalnica = $("select[name=poslovalnica]", $elm).val();
                    var vrsta = $("select[name=vrsta]", $elm).val();
                    $(".w-menu-gridThumb", $elm).each(function () {
                        $(this).show();
                        if (poslovalnica != "all" && $(this).data("poslovalnica") != poslovalnica) {
                            $(this).hide();
                        }
                        if (vrsta != "all" && $(this).data("vrsta") != vrsta) {
                            $(this).hide();
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        });


    });

</script>
